Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a genetics algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 835. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 613/1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on genetics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 835. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 613. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the genetics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 494. The moduli space has dimension at most 972 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 494 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the genetics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 972. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
